Timestamp rule
Filesystem modification time is a recency and routing signal, not proof that a finding or authority state changed.
Access rule
public_full_text true guarantees a crawler-friendly static reading_url. access_state private_by_design means metadata only. A failed true reading URL is an access failure, not a privacy signal.

Research interior layers

  • Orientation and control — roles: read_first, router — Start-here documents, package routers and controlling orientation.
  • Findings and synthesis — roles: answer, synthesis, claim_audit — Research answers, cross-record synthesis and explicit claim testing.
  • Evidence and records — roles: evidence_index, factual_spine, source_object, source_ledger — Primary-source custody, factual spines, evidence indexes and source ledgers.
  • Monitoring and returns — roles: watchboard — Open clocks, return gates and monitored questions.
  • Methods and production — roles: method, builder, validator, tool, workflow_record — Methods, deterministic builders, validators, tools and workflow receipts.
  • Presentation and projections — roles: visual, generated_projection, deliverable — Human-facing visuals, generated projections and packaged deliverables.
  • Supporting material — roles: supporting, other — Supporting analysis and governed material not assigned to a narrower role.
